# Elf Destiny - community-designed DNA imported 2026-10 from the Discord elf-tinder
# and dark-elf-tinder channels (characters in the history/characters bloodline files and misc_characters.txt
# and, for Gromph, bloodline_syldranis_characters.txt). Converted from the submitters'
# ruler-designer / save exports: genes this mod does not define were dropped (CFP
# accessories, CBO body genes, AGOT dragon genes, empty save-export slots), four
# AGOT-only templates were remapped to the closest vanilla ones (Frieren, Telperion),
# and, as for the first dark-elf-tinder batch, accidental normal_skin/normal_hair
# recessives on dark elves were replaced by the dominant drow template so pale
# descendants need a real light ancestor. Full conversion log in the modkit
# import report.
